All these new claims remain UNVERIFIED.\n\nThe FULL joint13c7d6e297580087 removes constant-gradient restrictions inside its scalar inward blocks, but its whole-class bound still assumes the prescribed first-occurrence checkpoints and disjoint same-ray visits. Closed components and obsolete routes\n\nSigned-closed components:\n- All-n12/5 benchmark envelope and SAME-m47/50 upper comparison; full-function implication remains conditional — fact_id931b9653ce0f7201.\n- Exact full-prefix maximal-value construction and terminal-square conventions — fact_id1252b8823bb5a290.\n- Full scalar Huber/cut channels and original all-split benchmark definitions — fact_idb98bb807d514e80d.\n- Scoped small-amplitude spiral obstruction, not a full-class upper — fact_id5fd396eba75651e7.\n- Actual static multistage completions and special-word comparisons — fact_idc3bfc54321ddec65.\n\n| Route | Status | Scoped reason |\n|---|---|---|\n| Treating a fixed homogeneous finite library as a universal exact-profile proof | FALSE AS STATED | fact_id0be8b54e0a33381c gives its finite-library limitation |\n| Reoptimizing only the accepted small-amplitude spiral class | OBSOLETE | fact_id5fd396eba75651e7 excludes its intended benchmark attainment in the declared scope |\n\nNew full shared drafts are awareness, not signed-closed components. d031c723cebd5dde constructs three destination/scale-dependent laws: its even-depth law pays old named screens but fails another complete original direction, and the origin relay loses its first diagonal. bb1edc657a2239e2 treats two specified nonacute outside starts and an arbitrary-rank ACUTE normal family, leaving general nonacute/dependent dictionaries open. eea0512960806ccc proposes a uniform actual nonlinear exclusion of the displayed arbitrary-coefficient equal-basic-module F/M/E layouts. Its scope is that family; independent acceptance is absent. These reports do not supply a W upper or an arbitrary-word theorem.\n\nThe full joint draft25e480a41fc81415 supplies a legal explicit all-word portfolio: one selected top-two positive-height triangle, its complete at-most-six-anchor prefix completion, and quadratic-prefix completions at fixed curvatures1 and1/2, augmented by accepted K and exact recognized c3bfc special words. It also proves, within this UNVERIFIED draft, that the precise portfolio remains at the canonical Huber value on every same-enclosing-m H_(m,1/4), m>=1536, with original ratio<237/250. Thus legal all-word realization alone is insufficient. A multiscale or growing dictionary, different partition/height law, or lossless combinatorial comparison remains outside this scope. No all-n theorem or unrestricted upper follows.\n\nTwo further complete shared drafts sharpen the scope boundary.5100e05d4c62d3ff includes EVERY scalar cut in a growing orthogonal dictionary, arbitrary weights/subsets/repetitions, and a synchronized rather than separable complete-prefix maximum. It still derives a whole-family same-m H_(m,1/4) deficit through the actual final queried value and gradient. This is UNVERIFIED; it excludes neither arbitrary nonlinear scalar prefixes nor general correlated gradient sectors.2630258977073049 proposes a uniform actual physical exclusion of its unequal-middle module law and all same-length/same-mass internal rearrangements. Its last two gradients remain opposite collinear vectors. That is a scoped draft exclusion, not an all-word reduction or an optimum theorem for general three-gradient Gram geometry.\n\nThe full UNVERIFIED9ceef38b9c3f0498 report excludes two complete global circulation generators and their nonnegative mixtures on the inherited base, on every large-m original zero-displacement wave. Its contact identity also shows why a nonnegative balanced finite circulation is generally indefinite. It does not exclude paid signed reallocation, changed optimum profiles or other bases. The full historical numerical reportad24079f80cd6a3a has Huber-like full PEP results only at m3..6, with actual negative numerical eigenvalues retained; it supplies neither exact feasibility nor asymptotic evidence. The old Newton/Brownian kernel83a03447bbd73458 concerns a prescribed temporal quotient, not the full original certificate space.\n\nThe full UNVERIFIED8bf359c98a737933 report makes its centered Walsh simplex scalar only on an explicitly two-group barycentric initial line. Its carried nonacute LawsII/III impose guards that prevent positive-height reactivation. General asymmetric multi-group starts of the CENTERED simplex lie outside those scopes. The completed direct growing-facet draft with one shared and fresh orthogonal coordinates also has a whole-law deficit; generic dimension growth is not a missing constant-level construction.\n\nThe FULL joint36815a66748f5402 has an exact seven-endpoint original-query lift for three constant-gradient sectors with arbitrary real vectors, heights and initial geometry. Its explicit five-row certificate proves the Huber optimum only for pairs among the THREE largest checkpoints of SAME-m H_(m,1/4), and a free-terminal completed-family ratio<316/333. The certificate's determinant has a negative limiting value for the actual whole root followed by its next leaf. This is an UNVERIFIED failure of that certificate, not a stronger legal primal instance. The exact full endpoint system remains available at this concrete escape pair and at other pairs. No family upper is a full-class W upper without a separate lossless reduction.\n\nThe FULL jointf7f9930b98aa7caa gives an actual centered-simplex inverse-fold construction with positive heights at every query and real support reentries. Its whole Cartesian law, even after every complete-prefix continuation and own-radius normalization, loses exponentially on SAME-m H_(m,1/4). The proof requires both inverse branches above EVERY future coordinate. Its exact3-level first-leaf counterexample refutes extending that width inequality to correlated starts. Section11 gives a correlated one-step inverse formula but no whole-history selector, controlled initial radius or terminal gain. All these new claims remain UNVERIFIED.\n\nThe FULL joint13c7d6e297580087 removes constant-gradient restrictions inside its scalar inward blocks, but its whole-class bound still assumes the prescribed first-occurrence checkpoints and disjoint same-ray visits. Its exact quadratic example(1,6,1,6,1) has overlapping returns and refutes deleting that spatial hypothesis. Arbitrary scalar returns remain open. A two-sided curvature law would preserve one global scalar derivative while changing the sign-dependent physical recurrence; a recurrence alone would not supply a quantitative original-profile result.\n\nThe FULL jointaadb47e847b5725f reports two paid dyadic optimum-profile transports and their complete original-space failures. Its Section6 identifies a consequential constraint: with xi=0 and exact Ce=D*nu/2, the full residual R=C-D*nu*nu^T/4 has the common vector in its kernel; a negative direction can be shifted to zero nu-coupling and defeats EVERY inflated q. Therefore exact contact cannot be imposed as a harmless normalization on a search that intends to spend objective slack. The numerical arrays only at m<=6 have altered optimum profiles and incoming terminal mass, with retained defects and poor preregistered fit errors; they provide no exact/asymptotic certificate. A useful new law must pay its actual nonzero contact or other endpoint price on the full original space.\n\nAccepted fact931b9653ce0f7201 proves the complete all-n all-split envelopes C_n<=(n+1)^rho and B_n<=(12/5)(n+1)^rho, and the SAME-m H_(m,1/4) comparison B_(2^m-1)/D_m<47/50 for every m>=1536. Its final799/800 minimax implication is CONDITIONAL on a separate full-function upper17/(16D_m), which remains absent. This scalar package does not solve the original target.\n\nFULL35290b8fa6113ce5 excludes every preceding-leaf pair weight and every equal-within-cell fan addition on the inherited base; the old H4 mode is decisive even with paid contact. Interface contracts\n\n| Interface | Required input | Quantitative output needed | Missing bridge | Status |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| Full original upper certificate | Nonnegative original multipliers, exact value flow, every independent vector, actual last leaf and terminal | W_n(H_m)<=17/(16D_m), or another rigorous benchmark-beating level | A new paid remote-row law and complete all-depth PSD | UNKNOWN |\n| Nonisotropic finite-field adversary | One fixed real body, Euclidean metric, whole chronological trajectory, minimum and own radius | Explicit attained normalized terminal gain on H_m or a complete whole-law obstruction | Joint geometry and quantitative all-history estimate | PARTIAL |\n| Asymmetric scalar adversary | One fixed monotone1-Lipschitz clipped derivative, all amplitudes/signs and threshold returns | Useful attained gain or uniform complete family profile | Odd symmetric envelope no longer applies | PARTIAL |\n| Arbitrary checkpoint geometry | All ordered endpoint rows and exact original-query lift for every pair | Whole three-sector profile, including every free terminal | LCA/dyadic classification and fully paid bidirectional certificate | PARTIAL |\n\nEven closure of the last three restricted-family interfaces is not an unrestricted W upper or a uniform all-word lower theorem. The missing original-target implication must be supplied separately.\n\n# 3. Dangerous shortcuts\n\nDo not turn an E80-feasible transcript into a function: its named missing row is violated. Do not treat a symmetric row pair or circulation as PSD; its displacement term is indefinite. Do not preserve a failed contact equation while spending objective slack. Do not infer whole-space positivity from finitely many modes or a temporal quotient.\n\nDo not identify Walsh orthogonality or expected Gram matrices with one real trajectory. A diagonal anisotropy is not an innocuous coordinate change under Euclidean GD. Pay every carrier, extreme coordinate, positive conjugate height and true initial radius. Do not assume identical coordinates split spontaneously.\n\nDo not export a symmetric-Huber radial argument to asymmetric or scaled curvatures without proving the changed envelope. Do not optimize child start points independently. Do not reset enclosing-m parameters at subtrees. Do not identify a three-sector endpoint certificate with full independent-gradient PSD.\n\nEvery benchmark comparison uses ALL integer splits; the UPPER bound B_n/D_m<47/50 cannot establish a lower witness's gain by reversal. No finite scan, exact recurrence alone, exponent-only result, conditional matrix inequality or restricted-family bound completes the original target.\n\n# 4. Missing bridge lemmas\n\nThe positive bridge is an actual selector V(h)<=W_n(h) for every finite h>=0 with B_n V(h)>=1-o(1) uniformly at every sufficiently large n. One static smooth convex function, original chronology, complete minimum and true radius are required. This retains theorem-level strength.\n\nThe negative bridge is an explicit nonnegative original-row certificate (or other full-function argument) on actual unbounded words with a fixed benchmark improvement. The exact full-space Schur condition and its all-depth coefficients must be proved, not posed as an unknown SDP. Accepted931 supplies only the final scalar comparison once such a bound exists.\n\nThe four current interfaces are decisive tests of different mechanisms for those bridges. Exact obstructions should name their entire parameter domains and what remains outside. Neither a legal construction without quantitative gain nor a relaxation counterexample without interpolation may be described as a solved bridge.\n", "verifiable": false, "status": "open", "fact_id": null, "links": {"finding_ids": ["05423f48b823e3bf", "bac703f383b0fbee"], "fact_ids": ["1252b8823bb5a290", "b98bb807d514e80d", "931b9653ce0f7201"], "original_target": "OPEN", "new_formal_allowance": 0}, "glossary": {}}
{"id": "a7f4699df96e7a81", "timestamp_utc": "2026-10-02T18:28:10.854695+00:00", "author": "main_agent", "kind": "elaboration", "claim": "Not solved. Two remote-row laws fail on complete original vectors; joint terminal pooling and full-space square-factor synthesis remain a different upper-certificate interface.", "evidence": "# 0. Mathematical verdict\n\n**Not solved.** The original uniform all-word lower comparison and a competitive full-function upper certificate are both absent. The target is exactly lim_n W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1 or a rigorous disproof, with W=2R, all predetermined nonnegative words, all finite dimensions, and an attained minimum. The affirmative quantifiers are every epsilon>0, one N_epsilon independent of h, every n>=N_epsilon, every h>=0. A negative result requires a fixed relative gap on unbounded actual horizons and an upper bound on the FULL function class.\n\nAccepted fact931b9653ce0f7201 establishes C_n<=(n+1)^rho, B_n<=(12/5)(n+1)^rho for all integer splits, and B_(2^m-1)/D_m<47/50 for the SAME-enclosing-m H_(m,1/4), every m>=1536. Its799/800 separation is conditional on the still-missing full W<=17/(16D_m). Accepted1252b8823bb5a290 gives original ordered interpolation and full terminal completion. Acceptedb98bb807d514e80d fixes the benchmark, normalization and actual Huber/cut lower channels. These facts do not solve the target.\n\nThe latest drafts identify four different quantitative interfaces: missing remote row orientations in full-space upper certificates; real nonisotropic finite-field projection dynamics; asymmetric nonlinear scalar return dynamics; and arbitrary three-sector checkpoint geometry. Every new claim in those drafts remains UNVERIFIED.\n\n# 1. Closed components and obsolete routes\n\nThe benchmark/normalization and interpolation components above are CLOSED at their accepted scopes. The original positive and negative targets remain PARTIAL/UNKNOWN. Accepted5fd396eba75651e7 excludes only its small-amplitude spiral family; acceptedc3bfc54321ddec65 realizes specified special word families only.\n\nNew awareness, not accepted premises:\n- FULL0073a83e08ff2e21 changes both old bases but proves an obstruction for EVERY coefficient law supported on E80: all checkpoint pairs, all leaf-to-checkpoint rows, both adjacent directions, both optimum directions and the whole outgoing terminal fan. A complete scalar transcript satisfies those rows and requires q>2 for every m>=1536. It violates the omitted remote PREFIX row Q_(n-2,0)>0. Thus the draft is neither a legal primal lower bound nor unrestricted dual infeasibility. Any useful continuation must add actual missing remote checkpoint-to-leaf or other excluded rows and pay their indefinite costs on the full independent-gradient space.\n- FULLe9278cb7e2f8f4f9 constructs real rotating three-group simplex supports with positive heights at EVERY query, but its full q>=r, terminal-in-triangle family obeys E<=1/(D_m-8) and B_n E<19/20. Both count and terminal restrictions are load-bearing. General anisotropic bodies and non-carried dictionaries remain outside that scope; equal-group rotation alone is not an asymptotic gain.\n- FULLbac703f383b0fbee gives the candidate exact ALL-amplitude unit-curvature symmetric-Huber profile sup_s 2Hub(Psi_m(s))/s^2=1/D_m on every same-m H_m, m>=1536. Its radial interval-image proof crucially uses oddness, equal clipping thresholds and unit core curvature. General asymmetric/curvature-scaled nonlinear scalar returns are NOT covered. It does not automatically promote the separate copied-simplex reduction2993fddcc4a03577 into a fact.\n- FULL37987ddad8be4c0b uses a paid backward cycle, reselected contact weights and a dyadic classification to cover EVERY whole-root-SECOND three-sector pair. Fixed terminal is exactly Huber; every legal free terminal obeys V<=1/(D_m-d0^2), B_n V<940/999. Its earlier root-FIRST companion6b8cd30a96a9165b is likewise UNVERIFIED. Pairs neither of which is the whole root and changing within-sector gradients remain outside these scopes.\n\nLatest FULL public drafts sharpen two boundaries.05423f48b823e3bf proves an exact asymmetric unit-core Huber profile1/D_m ONLY when max(b_plus,b_minus)/min(b_plus,b_minus)<=1/(c-2), plus the exact one-flat-curvature profile for every nonnegative word. Its actual root escape disproves unrestricted weighted absorption but has optimized score<3/(4D_m). The signed OBJECTIVE-energy endpoint inequalities remain open for unrestricted positive ratios; independent positive curvatures are unattempted. dc755a6e62f17395 excludes two complete anisotropic Walsh laws: a quantitatively thin mixed-character perturbation for arbitrary starts/ranks, and ALL equal-norm vertex-query itineraries on the whole hierarchy (including huge bridges and a free actual terminal). Thick nonvertex queries are genuinely outside those scopes, with explicit noncommuting disphenoid edges. Both reports are UNVERIFIED.\n\nThe main direct draft e967ef4e5752cf2f has an independent no-gap-at-declared-scope audit recorded in d12c90331a4d3e5d, but remains UNVERIFIED. It approximates every finite smooth-convex GD run by a compact polytope projection-gradient run with one moving extra coordinate and a nearby POLAR-CONE minimizer. Its positive outgoing-optimum slack generally persists even at the closest minimizer. It therefore does not justify restricting to centered bodies or origin-tight normalization. The new sparse candidate5178ca3dbb6aece4 proposes at most n+2 vertices with exact horizontal dynamics for strict smoothness; a genuine-simplex perturbation is being investigated, not assumed proved. These structural candidates start from an already specified run and supply no schedule-only adversary or target-profile comparison.\n\nFULL9da28a701244ea55 gives exact failures for two remote-row laws. The entire contact-routed final-leaf fan fails by a common-adjusted long-step coordinate, independently of q. The dense earlier-leaf kernel fails on two equal-scale checkpoint gradients; its routed support requires h_r*Omega_p(r)<=2*sqrt(t_r/t_p), while the chosen kernel leaks too much. Both laws concentrate terminal flow at the last queried leaf and neither pays the reset transcript. These are scoped UNVERIFIED failures, not unrestricted dual infeasibility. A new upper search should jointly design terminal pooling, full initial/common costs and a complete-space PSD factor, rather than keep either routed law fixed.\n\nThe full direct sparse/simplex result a753ad89e79ede74 is a JOINT UNVERIFIED representation candidate: for each word with p positive steps, the full W supremum can be approached by projection-gradient functions of a simplex with0 as a vertex, <=p+2 vertices in dimension<=p+1. Query gradients can all be vertices with UNEQUAL norms. The complete polar minimum cone is essential. This starts from an already chosen adverse run; its schedule-only quantitative selector remains missing. Neither it nor its unequal-norm inverse-selection advisory resolves a lower comparison.\n\n# 2. No finite scan, exact recurrence alone, exponent-only result, conditional matrix inequality or restricted-family bound completes the original target.\n\n# 4. These structural candidates start from an already specified run and supply no schedule-only adversary or target-profile comparison.\n\nFULL9da28a701244ea55 gives exact failures for two remote-row laws. The entire contact-routed final-leaf fan fails by a common-adjusted long-step coordinate, independently of q. The dense earlier-leaf kernel fails on two equal-scale checkpoint gradients; its routed support requires h_r*Omega_p(r)<=2*sqrt(t_r/t_p), while the chosen kernel leaks too much. Both laws concentrate terminal flow at the last queried leaf and neither pays the reset transcript. These are scoped UNVERIFIED failures, not unrestricted dual infeasibility. A new upper search should jointly design terminal pooling, full initial/common costs and a complete-space PSD factor, rather than keep either routed law fixed.\n\nThe full direct sparse/simplex result a753ad89e79ede74 is a JOINT UNVERIFIED representation candidate: for each word with p positive steps, the full W supremum can be approached by projection-gradient functions of a simplex with0 as a vertex, <=p+2 vertices in dimension<=p+1. Query gradients can all be vertices with UNEQUAL norms. The complete polar minimum cone is essential. This starts from an already chosen adverse run; its schedule-only quantitative selector remains missing. Neither it nor its unequal-norm inverse-selection advisory resolves a lower comparison.\n\nFULL a79d801952174a0a covers EVERY three-sector pair with k outside the complete subtree rooted at j, including consecutive leftmost roots with their actual outer masses. Remaining ancestor--descendant pairs have an exact broader row table, contact equations and scalar determinant formula. The factor Delta=u^2*v^2*w-d^2*e^2*D is proved positive in that JOINT UNVERIFIED draft, but nonnegative multiplier clamping (10.5) is still unproved. The legal example at an uncovered pair is below Huber and is not a counterexample. Neither all-pair nor full W is established. A closed all-pair theorem would still need a separate changing-gradient/full-sector bridge.\n\nFULL21d26350c9141917 closes an acute two-height cone-simplex law exactly at1/D_m and a cap-dilated conic law at the much smaller product bound. Its general zero-query-normal-height screen is stronger than the chosen examples: if delta_i=<X_i-g_i,g_i>=0 at every queried point, the gradients are cone projections and the whole-word product obstruction holds, even with changing faces and strict outgoing-optimum slack about the closest minimum. Positive cap multiplier delta_i at some QUERY is necessary for improvement; nearby-minimum slack alone is insufficient. Obtuse, cap-active two-ray geometry remains open.\n\nFULL72141426b777f11c derives the exact all-amplitude one-sided clipping profile and the complete finite-ratio tail max/min>=Gamma_m=sqrt(2a d_(m-1)D_m). It also covers all zero/single-crossing histories and, if u>=d_(m-1)v, every start s<=u. The primitive inequality |x_n+S_m g_n|<=|s| is actually false. Intermediate finite ratios with repeated reactivation of the large clip remain open; objective-only child sublevel replacement loses the actual exterior history. Both reports are JOINT UNVERIFIED, not full W bounds.\n\n# 2. Interface contracts\n\n| Interface | Required input | Quantitative output needed | Missing bridge | Status |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| Full original upper certificate | Nonnegative original multipliers, exact value flow, every independent vector, actual last leaf and terminal | W_n(H_m)<=17/(16D_m), or another rigorous benchmark-beating level | A new paid remote-row law and complete all-depth PSD | UNKNOWN |\n| Nonisotropic finite-field adversary | One fixed real body, Euclidean metric, whole chronological trajectory, minimum and own radius | Explicit attained normalized terminal gain on H_m or a complete whole-law obstruction | Joint geometry and quantitative all-history estimate | PARTIAL |\n| Asymmetric scalar adversary | One fixed monotone1-Lipschitz clipped derivative, all amplitudes/signs and threshold returns | Useful attained gain or uniform complete family profile | Odd symmetric envelope no longer applies | PARTIAL |\n| Arbitrary checkpoint geometry | All ordered endpoint rows and exact original-query lift for every pair | Whole three-sector profile, including every free terminal | LCA/dyadic classification and fully paid bidirectional certificate | PARTIAL |\n\nEven closure of the last three restricted-family interfaces is not an unrestricted W upper or a uniform all-word lower theorem. The missing original-target implication must be supplied separately.\n\n# 3. Dangerous shortcuts\n\nDo not turn an E80-feasible transcript into a function: its named missing row is violated. Do not treat a symmetric row pair or circulation as PSD; its displacement term is indefinite. Do not preserve a failed contact equation while spending objective slack. Do not infer whole-space positivity from finitely many modes or a temporal quotient.\n\nDo not identify Walsh orthogonality or expected Gram matrices with one real trajectory. A diagonal anisotropy is not an innocuous coordinate change under Euclidean GD. Pay every carrier, extreme coordinate, positive conjugate height and true initial radius. Do not assume identical coordinates split spontaneously.\n\nDo not export a symmetric-Huber radial argument to asymmetric or scaled curvatures without proving the changed envelope. Do not optimize child start points independently. Do not reset enclosing-m parameters at subtrees. Do not identify a three-sector endpoint certificate with full independent-gradient PSD.\n\nEvery benchmark comparison uses ALL integer splits; the UPPER bound B_n/D_m<47/50 cannot establish a lower witness's gain by reversal. No finite scan, exact recurrence alone, exponent-only result, conditional matrix inequality or restricted-family bound completes the original target.\n\n# 4. Missing bridge lemmas\n\nThe positive bridge is an actual selector V(h)<=W_n(h) for every finite h>=0 with B_n V(h)>=1-o(1) uniformly at every sufficiently large n. One static smooth convex function, original chronology, complete minimum and true radius are required. This retains theorem-level strength.\n\nThe negative bridge is an explicit nonnegative original-row certificate (or other full-function argument) on actual unbounded words with a fixed benchmark improvement. The exact full-space Schur condition and its all-depth coefficients must be proved, not posed as an unknown SDP. Accepted931 supplies only the final scalar comparison once such a bound exists.\n\nThe four current interfaces are decisive tests of different mechanisms for those bridges. Exact obstructions should name their entire parameter domains and what remains outside. Neither a legal construction without quantitative gain nor a relaxation counterexample without interpolation may be described as a solved bridge.\n", "verifiable": false, "status": "open", "fact_id": null, "links": {"finding_ids": ["21d26350c9141917", "4039e33164ebf1c8"], "fact_ids": ["1252b8823bb5a290", "b98bb807d514e80d", "931b9653ce0f7201"], "original_target": "OPEN", "new_formal_allowance": 0}, "glossary": {}}

Interface contracts\n\n| Interface | Required input | Quantitative output needed | Missing bridge | Status |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| Full original upper certificate | Nonnegative original multipliers, exact value flow, every independent vector, actual last leaf and terminal | W_n(H_m)<=17/(16D_m), or another rigorous benchmark-beating level | A new paid remote-row law and complete all-depth PSD | UNKNOWN |\n| Nonisotropic finite-field adversary | One fixed real body, Euclidean metric, whole chronological trajectory, minimum and own radius | Explicit attained normalized terminal gain on H_m or a complete whole-law obstruction | Joint geometry and quantitative all-history estimate | PARTIAL |\n| Asymmetric scalar adversary | One fixed monotone1-Lipschitz clipped derivative, all amplitudes/signs and threshold returns | Useful attained gain or uniform complete family profile | Odd symmetric envelope no longer applies | PARTIAL |\n| Arbitrary checkpoint geometry | All ordered endpoint rows and exact original-query lift for every pair | Whole three-sector profile, including every free terminal | LCA/dyadic classification and fully paid bidirectional certificate | PARTIAL |\n\nEven closure of the last three restricted-family interfaces is not an unrestricted W upper or a uniform all-word lower theorem. The missing original-target implication must be supplied separately.\n\n# 3. Dangerous shortcuts\n\nDo not turn an E80-feasible transcript into a function: its named missing row is violated. Do not infer whole-space positivity from finitely many modes or a temporal quotient.\n\nDo not identify Walsh orthogonality or expected Gram matrices with one real trajectory. A diagonal anisotropy is not an innocuous coordinate change under Euclidean GD. Pay every carrier, extreme coordinate, positive conjugate height and true initial radius. Do not assume identical coordinates split spontaneously.\n\nDo not export a symmetric-Huber radial argument to asymmetric or scaled curvatures without proving the changed envelope. Do not optimize child start points independently. Do not reset enclosing-m parameters at subtrees. Do not identify a three-sector endpoint certificate with full independent-gradient PSD.\n\nEvery benchmark comparison uses ALL integer splits; the UPPER bound B_n/D_m<47/50 cannot establish a lower witness's gain by reversal. No finite scan, exact recurrence alone, exponent-only result, conditional matrix inequality or restricted-family bound completes the original target.\n\n# 4. Missing bridge lemmas\n\nThe positive bridge is an actual selector V(h)<=W_n(h) for every finite h>=0 with B_n V(h)>=1-o(1) uniformly at every sufficiently large n. One static smooth convex function, original chronology, complete minimum and true radius are required. This retains theorem-level strength.\n\nThe negative bridge is an explicit nonnegative original-row certificate (or other full-function argument) on actual unbounded words with a fixed benchmark improvement. The exact full-space Schur condition and its all-depth coefficients must be proved, not posed as an unknown SDP. Accepted931 supplies only the final scalar comparison once such a bound exists.\n\nThe four current interfaces are decisive tests of different mechanisms for those bridges. Exact obstructions should name their entire parameter domains and what remains outside. These are UNVERIFIED mathematical statements; neither family controls full W.\n\nLatest full-prefix development, joint UNVERIFIED: reportd134cc9e6dbd9769 proves the adaptive one-anchor selector on all zero-height legal prefixes in arbitrary dimension and on max_i delta_i<=||x0||^2/(12D sqrt(m)), using a fully bounded suffix-product sum e_m<6sqrt(m). It gives exact true full-prefix values on the root-sign quadratic and Huber, and a genuine two-dimensional passing family. The original all-positive-height correlation cover remains unproved and unrefuted. Height differences alter the original normal conditions; they cannot be erased while retaining the projection argument. The complete signed height telescope and a true proximal height function are potential new interfaces, not accepted results.\n\nLatest independent-common-column result762b936882b2b6bb is joint UNVERIFIED. It derives the entire original polynomial, all optimum moments, first-row total/clock and last-row penultimate-clock. Positive initial incoming load and an independent clock-zero column still fail under the two explicit laws: the endpoint/tail law fails the first clock, while terminal concentration plus a penultimate metric atom repairs that clock but forces a remote last-query negative multiplier. The remaining problem is simultaneous all-row matching, not a missing PSD factor alone. A terminal pool with clock exactly2 and a jointly chosen late metric fraction is a concrete new candidate, not a verified assertion.\n\n# 2. Interface contract table\n\n### Original full-space certificate\nInput required: every original Q_ij<=0, value cancellation, nonnegative multipliers, and PSD on independent x_0,g_0,...,g_n, with the initial charge paid once. Output claimed: W_n(h)<=q. Available facts:1252b8823bb5a290 gives the row orientation and terminal completion. Missing verification on the actual model: explicit coefficient law with all finite and optimum signs at q<=17/(16D_m). Status:PARTIAL.\n\n### Separate terminal and common directions\nInput required: original conjugate coordinates, explicit probabilities, independent common column z with e^Tz=p+s, explicit actual skew divergence and paid zero-sum imbalance, all original signs. Output claimed: a potential extension of the restricted matching architecture. Available facts:1252b8823bb5a290 only for original row algebra; the displayed extension in research/INDEPENDENT_COMMON_SQUARE_ADVISORY_20261003.md is not accepted. Missing verification: the joint full identity, global load/row moments and an actual sign-feasible law. Failure mode: treating a free common square as free transport or assuming its PSD implies interpolation signs. Status:PARTIAL.\n\n### Signed scalar energy\nInput required: one static clipped derivative with independent positive curvatures, all starts, exact thresholds and actual same-m children. Output claimed: a quantitative family objective profile or improving legal witness. Available facts:931b9653ce0f7201 only compares a subsequently proved bound with the benchmark. Missing verification: independent-curvature multiple crossings while retaining destination energy and caps. Failure mode: a weak curvature permits a large radius that is not a large objective. Status:PARTIAL.\n\n### Full triangle face changes\nInput required: the centered equilateral body, all initial positions and all original face guards, with the actual Euclidean denominator. Output claimed: a uniform quantitative profile or an explicit all-depth legal selector. Available facts:931b9653ce0f7201 supplies scalar benchmark comparison only. Missing verification: change-of-face recursion with tangential terms and simultaneous guards. Failure mode: optimizing incompatible children, discarding a transverse coordinate, or selecting a finite expression without bounding it. Status:PARTIAL.\n\n### Quantitative conclusion in the original model\nInput required: a full-class upper bound on the displayed H_m or a uniform all-word lower bound with the exact benchmark. Output claimed: disproof by fixed separation, or the requested limit. Available facts:931b9653ce0f7201 closes the negative scalar comparison after the missing hypothesis; b98bb807d514e80d fixes the positive normalization. Missing verification: the quantitative full-class inequality. Failure mode: restricted bodies/functions and finitely many horizons do not cover the goal. Status:SUBSTANTIAL.\n\n# 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue\n\n- DANGEROUS: 'Full PSD and optimum signs are enough.' Original finite row signs are a separate requirement;121bbbe4b6a46fe3 isolates a first-row clock obstruction after all those screens pass.\n- DANGEROUS: 'The larger curvature controls every radius.' The explicit clipped counterexample in7476ebfe7be45713 retains radius while paying small objective curvature. A correct energy statement must retain the terminal side.\n- DANGEROUS: 'Every fixed face is Huber-optimal, so changing faces are too.' Actual projection maps are nonlinear across guards and tangential motion survives a bridge.\n- DANGEROUS: 'Degree at most8 means the uniform triangle maximum is known.' The maximizing face history still ranges over the full original word.\n- DANGEROUS: 'Simplex representation supplies a schedule-only lower witness.' The representation starts from an already chosen run, with a full polar minimum cone.\n- DANGEROUS: 'A failed formal PSD direction is a convex-smooth counterexample.' All original interpolation rows still need to hold for a single static function.\n\nConcrete anti-routes: fixing a known impossible load law while retuning only balanced skew; replacing independent curvature by its maximum in a radial lemma; dropping the last leaf; resetting child m; propagating a convex hull through a nonlinear map without justification; calling scoped family results full W bounds. The remaining problem is simultaneous all-row matching, not a missing PSD factor alone. Its transport/variance cut excludes the full stated terminal-concentrated,source-heavy metric family with the clock-zero source/end common column. Its strict-source lift preserves horizontal dynamics and the true initial radius exactly;regularization covers every original selected minimizer.
